# Agents · F · part 2

1,034 entries, fact-checker to falco-expert. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fact-checker**: Diffs every claim rendered on the portfolio site against the approved facts in .claude/skills/portfolio-facts/SKILL.md and lists anything on the site that the facts do not support. Use at the end of runbook Phases 4 to 10 and after any edit to content, data files, hero chips, metrics, dates, links, or contact details. Read-only; never edits. ([SrujanRudrapati/srujan-portfolio](https://github.com/SrujanRudrapati/srujan-portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: Use this agent during /rspec:audit to check a sample of a reverse spec's acceptance criteria against the public sources they cite (docs, changelog, API schema). Read-only on the spec; writes one report. &lt;example&gt; Context: the tech stage is done and the pipeline reaches the audit user: "/rspec:audit" assistant: "I'll draw the sample and launch the fact-checker alongside the coverage and consistency auditors." &lt;commentary&gt; Each auditor reads different things and writes its own report, so they run in parallel. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([tammai/rspec](https://github.com/tammai/rspec/blob/HEAD/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: Verifies that reworded CV prose asserts nothing more than the claims it cites — the semantic pass the mechanical fact gate cannot perform. Use when checking a tailored CV for overclaiming or scope inflation. ([tareksamni/my-cv-builder](https://github.com/tareksamni/my-cv-builder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: Verify that an authored docs page's prose claims match the cited source code. ([theoju/engineering-docs-agent](https://github.com/theoju/engineering-docs-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: 리포트의 핵심 주장을 외부 사실 대비 검증하는 Devil's Advocate 에이전트. 과장·오류·미확인 주장을 식별한다. ([TI-CTO/ti-intel](https://github.com/TI-CTO/ti-intel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: 합성된 주장(claim) 1개를 회의론자 관점에서 적대적으로 검증해 refute 여부·신뢰도·근거를 판정하는 검증 전문가. perspective-diverse fan-out 대상. 판단 품질 필요 — opus 티어. ([tobyilee/charlie-harness](https://github.com/tobyilee/charlie-harness/blob/HEAD/examples/research-harness/.claude/agents/fact-checker.md))

- **fact-checker**: 執筆ユニットの事実確認の検査専任（Haiku 検査級・読み取り専用）。数値・固有名詞・日付・法規に触れる断定・AIO や上位記事と矛盾する記述・出典の無い統計・[要出典] マーカーを洗い出し、「要修正（根拠）/ 要出典 / 要人間判断 / OK」で返す。修正はしない。Use when procedures/seo-write.md の手順4 で完成ユニットごとに委譲するとき。Not for 文章品質・SEO 観点の評価（→ keyword-gate / fix-integrator）、執筆（→ unit-drafter）。 ([UNISON-TECHNOLOGY/seo-content-worker](https://github.com/UNISON-TECHNOLOGY/seo-content-worker/blob/HEAD/agents/fact-checker.md) · 2 copies)

- **fact-checker**: Delegate to this subagent for the ENTAILED-mode check inside gate G10 CLAIM_TRACEABILITY, whenever a narration or on-screen sentence is marked paraphrase_of a source rather than verbatim. Always invoke it in a fresh, isolated context containing only {sentence, source_text} — never the beat, the plan, brief.goal, or which verdict would be convenient — because its entire value is that it cannot be talked into agreeing. ([yousuf7474/demo-studio](https://github.com/yousuf7474/demo-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fact-checker.md))
- **fact-checker**: You are generating a metastate agent prompt. You will produce a complete, production-ready agent markdown file that follows the metastate agent structure contract exactly. ([yueliang13/metastate](https://github.com/yueliang13/metastate/blob/HEAD/dist/research/agents/fact-checker.md) · 2 copies)
- **fact-checker-agent**: Use this agent when you need to verify claims, sources, and assertions in research documents, reports, or any content where factual accuracy is critical. Operates in two modes: proactive verification (default) checks claims via web search and real-time source evaluation; retrospective verification checks documents against pre-collected local sources with deterministic traceability. Primary use: verifying research findings before they propagate into artefact production. Secondary use: verifying new claims introduced during artefact production. ([isvlasov/rageatc-oss](https://github.com/isvlasov/rageatc-oss/blob/HEAD/plugins/rageatc-core-oss/agents/fact-checker-agent.md) · ★11)

- **fact-verifier**: Fact-verification specialist for the AI/Tech newsletter.
